% Answer
?- route(a, f, Path, Cost).
Path = [a, c, b, d, e, f], Cost = 13.

?- route(f, a, Path, Cost).
Path = [f, e, d, b, c, a], Cost = 13.

?- route(a, a, Path, Cost).
Path = [a], Cost = 0.
